\n\n\n\n\n\n\n当某角色的技能总值为全队最高时，该数值会变成金色。	\n\n\n\n\n\n\nWhen a character's Skill has the highest total value of anyone in the party the total will turn Gold.

+ 符号表示该物品会随时间再生。	A + symbol indicates that the item will regenerate in time.
1 格外伤害减半	Half damage at range 1	1
<color=#FFE017>先攻：\r</color>\n\n先攻决定战斗开始时你的角色行动的顺序。它先由你的速度值决定，再受队伍阵型修正。\n\n阵型可在「属性」与「抗性」按钮之间的阵型菜单中调整。 \n\n你也可以用「推迟回合」按钮延后你的回合。	<color=#FFE017>Initiative:\r</color>\n\nInitiative determines the order in which your character goes at the start of combat. It is determined first, by your Speed value, and then modified by your formation.\n\nFormation can be modified in the Formation Menu, available in-between the Stats and Resist buttons. \n\nYour turn can also be delayed with the Delay Turn button.
<color=#FFE017>力量：\r</color>\n+ 武器伤害\r\n+ 抵抗物理\r\n+ 抵抗恐惧\r\n+ 生命值（次要）\r\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 徒手格斗\r\n+ 单手武器\r\n+ 双手武器\r\n+ 远程武器	<color=#FFE017>Strength:\r</color>\n+ Weapon Damage\r\n+ Resisting Physical\r\n+ Resisting Fear\r\n+ Hit Points (secondary)\r\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Hand-To-Hand Weapons\r\n+ One-Handed Weapons\r\n+ Two-Handed Weapons\r\n+ Ranged Weapons
<color=#FFE017>力量：\r</color>\n+ 武器伤害\r\n+ 抵抗物理\r\n+ 抵抗恐惧\r\n+ 生命值（次要）\r\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 徒手格斗\r\n+ 单手武器\r\n+ 双手武器\r\n+ 远程武器\n\n<color=#FFE017>属性如何生效：\r</color>\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Strength:\r</color>\n+ Weapon Damage\r\n+ Resisting Physical\r\n+ Resisting Fear\r\n+ Hit Points (secondary)\r\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Hand-To-Hand Weapons\r\n+ One-Handed Weapons\r\n+ Two-Handed Weapons\r\n+ Ranged Weapons\n\n<color=#FFE017>How Stats work:\r</color>\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>升级：\r</color>\n\n等级代表你角色的成长程度。赢得战斗或完成任务时，你会获得经验值。\n当经验值足够高时，经验条会填满，你将提升一级。随后经验条重置，你也会回复全部失去的生命与法力。\n升级时你还会获得：\n+ 技能点——可在技能菜单中投入，以提升技能等级。\n<color=#FFBF00>+ 能力点 </color>——可在能力菜单中购买新能力。\n能力点每 5 级发放一次，以你头像旁的橙色标记表示。\n<color=#00BFFF>+ 特质点 </color>——可在特质菜单中购买新特质。\n特质点每 3 级发放一次，以你头像旁的蓝色标记表示。\n当你通过「怪物学识」对某种怪物了解得足够多时，即可选择新的特质。\n<color=#F50002>+ 最大生命 </color>——获得的生命点数等于你的抗性点数除以 10，加上力量点数的一半除以 10。\n<color=#005BF5>+ 最大法力 </color>——获得的法力点数等于你的心智点数除以 10，加上精神点数的一半除以 10。\n	<color=#FFE017>Leveling:\r</color>\n\nLevels are how far your character has progressed. When battles are won or quests are completed, you are awarded Experience Points.\nWhen these points are high enough, your experience bar will be full and you will gain a level. The experience bar will then reset and you will regain all missing Health and Mana.\nWhen you level up you also gain:\n+ Skill Points - These can be aded in the Skills menu to raise skill levels.\n<color=#FFBF00>+ Ability Points </color>- These can be used to purchase new Abilities under the Abilities menu.\nAbility Points are awarded every 5 levels and are represented by an orange mark near your portrait.\n<color=#00BFFF>+ Trait Points </color>- These can be used to purchase new Traits under the Traits menu.\nTrait Points are awarded every 3 levels and are represented by a blue mark near your portrait.\nNew traits become availble to choose when you learn enough about a monster through Monster Lore.\n<color=#F50002>+ Max Health </color> - Health points gained are equal to your Resistance points over 10 plus half your Strength points over 10.\n<color=#005BF5>+ Max Mana </color> -  Mana points gained are equal to your Mind points over 10 plus half your Spirit points over 10.\n
<color=#FFE017>反应：\r</color>\n\n反应让你有机会躲开某些能力、抵抗定身，并在本会受伤时避开陷阱。	<color=#FFE017>Reflex:\r</color>\n\nReflex allows you an opportunity to dodge certain abilities, resist being rooted and avoid traps when you would otherwise be damaged.
<color=#FFE017>命中：\r</color>\n\n命中以 1-20 的随机掷骰开始。\n随后将你的命中值，以及你因了解该类怪物而获得的学识加成，加到掷值上。\n\n再用你的命中掷值对抗防御者的闪避掷值。若达到或超过对方的数值，即命中。\n\n若你的回合中未移动，命中提高 10%。\n	<color=#FFE017>To Hit:\r</color>\n\nHit attempts begin with a random roll of 1-20.\nYour ToHit value is then added to that roll along with any Lore Boost you may have from learning about that type of monster.\n\nYour ToHit roll is then matched against a defenders Dodge roll. If you match or beat their value then you have scored a hit.\n\nToHit is increased by 10% if you have not moved on your turn.\n
<color=#FFE017>心智：\r</color>\n+ 抵抗恐惧\n+ 抗心智\n+ 法力值（主要）\n+ 技能点\n+ 更快学会技能、能力与法术\n+ 决定最高法术层级（若高于 SPI）\n\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 警觉\n+ 怪物学识\n+ 生存\n+ 卸除武器\n+ 鉴定\n+ 议价\n\n<color=#FFE017>属性如何生效：\r</color>\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Mind:\r</color>\n+ Resisting Fear\n+ Resist Mind\n+ Mana Points (primary)\n+ Skill Points\n+ Learn Skills, Abilities and Spells Faster\n+ Determines max Spell Tier (if higher than SPI)\n\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Awareness\n+ Monster Lore\n+ Survival\n+ Disarm\n+ Identify\n+ Barter\n\n<color=#FFE017>How Stats work:\r</color>\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>意志力：\r</color>\n\n意志力用于将自己的意志强加于人，或抵抗他人对你意志的扭转。试图让你违背意愿移动、或威吓你的能力与法术都以它结算。	<color=#FFE017>Willpower:\r</color>\n\nWillpower is used to force your will upon others, or to resist having your will overturned. Abilities or Spells that attempt to move you against your will or to Intimidate you use this.
<color=#FFE017>抗性：\r</color>\n+ 生命值（主要）\n+ 意志力\n+ 抵抗物理\n+ 抵抗魔法\n+ 防御\n\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 生存\n+ 急救\n\n<color=#FFE017>属性如何生效：\r</color>\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Resistance:\r</color>\n+ Hit Points (primary)\n+ Willpower\n+ Resist Physical\n+ Resist Magic\n+ Defense\n\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Survival\n+ First Aid\n\n<color=#FFE017>How Stats work:\r</color>\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>护甲：\r</color>\n\n护甲提供稳定的伤害减免，但对无视护甲的攻击（例如酸蚀）无效。\n\n若你受到 10 点伤害而拥有 5 点护甲，则只会承受 5 点伤害。	<color=#FFE017>Armor:\r</color>\n\nArmor is guaranteed damage reduction except against attacks that ignore Armor, such as Acid.\n\nIf you are dealt 10 points of damage and have 5 Armor, you will take only 5 damage.
<color=#FFE017>敏捷：\r</color>\n+ 命中\n+ 反应\n+ 抗心智\n+ 闪避\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 回避\n+ 远程武器\n+ 卸除武器\n+ 潜行\n+ 制作\n\n<color=#FFE017>属性如何生效：\r</color>\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Agility:\r</color>\n+ To Hit\n+ Reflex\n+ Resist Mind\n+ Dodge\n\r\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Evasion\n+ Ranged Weapons\n+ Disarm\n+ Stealth\n+ Craft\n\n<color=#FFE017>How Stats work:\r</color>\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>法力：\r</color>\n\n这根条代表你拥有的法力（法力值）。升级时最大法力值会提高。\n\n提高量等于你的心智点数除以 10，再加上精神点数的一半除以 10。	<color=#FFE017>Mana:\r</color>\n\nThis bar represents how much Mana, or Mana Points, you have. Your maximum Mana Points are increased when you gain a level.\n\nThe amount increased is equal to your Mind points over 10 plus half your Spirit points over 10.
<color=#FFE017>生命：\r</color>\n\n这根条代表你拥有的生命（生命值）。升级时最大生命值会提高。\n\n提高量等于你的抗性点数除以 10，再加上力量点数的一半除以 10。\n	<color=#FFE017>Health:\r</color>\n\nThis bar represents how much Health, or Hit Points, you have. Your maximum Hit Points are increased when you gain a level.\n\nThe amount increased is equal to your Resistance points over 10 plus half your Strength points over 10.\n
<color=#FFE017>移动：\r</color>\n\n移动力决定你在战斗中能移动多少格。\n\n移动力等于你的速度 ÷ 3（四舍五入）。	<color=#FFE017>Movement:\r</color>\n\nMovement determines how many tiles you can move in combat.\n\nMovement is equal to your Speed / 3, (rounded).
<color=#FFE017>精神：\r</color>\n+ 法术伤害/强度\n+ 法力值（次要）\n+ 抵抗魔法\n+ 决定最高法术层级（若高于 MND）\n\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 音乐\n+ 鉴定\n+ 制作\n+ 徒手格斗\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Spirit:\r</color>\n+ Spell Damage/Intensity\n+ Mana Points (secondary)\n+ Resist Magic\n+ Determines max Spell Tier (if higher than MND)\n\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Music\n+ Identify\n+ Craft\n+ Hand to Hand Weapons\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>经验：\r</color>\n\n经验值让角色得以升级。赢得战斗或完成任务时，你会获得经验值。\n当经验值足够高时，经验条会填满，你将提升一级。随后经验条重置，你也会回复全部失去的生命与法力。\n\n如需更具体的信息，请查看「等级」提示。	<color=#FFE017>Experience:\r</color>\n\nExperience points allow your characters to gain levels. When battles are won or quests are completed, you are awarded Experience Points.\nWhen these points are high enough, your experience bar will be full and you will gain a level. The experience bar will then reset and you will regain all missing Health and Mana.\n\nFor more specific information, look at the Level tooltip.
<color=#FFE017>速度：\r</color>\n+ 先攻\n+ 移动速度\n+ 命中\n+ 闪避\n\n<color=#00BFBF>技能：\r</color>\n+ 警觉\n+ 回避\n+ 急救\n+ 音乐\n\n<color=#FFE017>属性如何生效：\r</color>\n\n属性以倍率方式生效，基准值为 10。每高出或低于 10 一点，修正值就变化 10%。因此，单点提升看似不多，实际上会随着你的成长带来越来越大的增益。	<color=#FFE017>Speed:\r</color>\n+ Initiative\n+ Movement Rate\n+ To Hit\n+ Dodge\n\n<color=#00BFBF>Skills:\r</color>\n+ Awareness\n+ Evasion\n+ First Aid\n+ Music\n\n<color=#FFE017>How Stats work:\r</color>\n\nStats work as multipliers, with 10 being the baseline. Every Stat value above or below that changes your modifier by 10%. In this regard, a single point increase may not look like much, but actually represents an increase that scales as you grow more powerful.
<color=#FFE017>闪避：\r</color>\n\n闪避是尝试完全无视一次攻击，使伤害计算根本不会发生。某些效果必须先命中才能生效，而闪避是避开它们的唯一办法。\n闪避以 1-20 的随机掷骰开始。\n\n随后将你的闪避值加到掷值上，与攻击者的命中值比较。\n若你超过对方的命中值，闪避成功。	<color=#FFE017>Dodge:\r</color>\n\nDodge is an attempt to ignore an attack entirely, so that no damage calculation is made. Some effects require a hit, and Dodge is the only way to avoid them.\nDodge attempts begin with a random roll of 1-20.\n\nYour Dodge value is then added to that roll and compared to the attackers ToHit value.\nIf you beat the ToHit value you have successfully dodged.
<color=#FFE017>防御：\r</color>\n\n防御是一项 50/50 的判定，可减免任何类型的物理伤害，包括无视护甲的伤害。\n\n防御在你的护甲减免伤害之后结算，作为第二层防护。	<color=#FFE017>Defense:\r</color>\n\nDefense is a 50/50 chance to reduce physical damage of any type, including damage that ignores armor.\n\nDefense is calculated after your armor reduces incoming damage and acts as a secondary defense.
<i>莱伊：\r</i>\n\r\n整个埃梅里斯克只出过一个莱伊：Atticus·莱伊。我之所以把他收录进来，是因为围绕着他的事件颇为值得注意。在那个被一些人称为理性与稳定时代的时期，埃梅里斯克出了个疯子。他自称巫师，却拿不出任何证据。直到死去之前，人们常能看到他在墓地的西北角胡言乱语。 	<i>Ley:\r</i>\n\r\nThere has only been one Ley in all of Emerisk: Atticus Ley. I have chosen to include him because the surrounding events are notable. In what some would call an era of reason and stability, Emerisk had a madman. He claimed to be a wizard, but failed to produce any evidence of such claims. He was frequently found raving in the northwest corner of the graveyard, up until his death.

倒计时	Countdowns
倒计时：\n\n进行某些行动时，失败会产生一段倒计时，以红色进度条表示。计时器每剩下一秒，后续掷骰就会被扣减相应点数。\n\n这是为了防止反复刷用卸除武器、鉴定之类的技能。\n\n	Countdowns:\n\nWhen performing some actions, a failure will result in a countdown, represented by a red bar. For every second left on the timer, additional rolls will be reduced by that many points.\n\nThis is to prevent spamming skills like Disarm and Identify.\n\n

先攻	Initiative
先攻加成	Initiative Bonus
先攻顺序	Initiative Order
先攻：	Initiative:
先攻：\r\n战斗开始时，每个角色与怪物都要进行一次先攻检定。先攻值最高者最先行动，依此类推。\r\n\n你的基础先攻值会先加上一个 -5 到 +5 的随机值。 \n\n队伍阵型可使你的先攻在 -3 到 +3 之间变化。\r\n先攻在下一场战斗前不会重新掷骰。\r	Initiative:\r\nAt the beginning of combat, each character and monster makes an initiative roll. The highest initiative takes the first turn, and so on.\r\n\nFirst, a random roll from -5 to +5 is added to your base Initiative. \n\nThe formation of the party can alter your initiative between -3 and +3.\r\nInitiative is not re-rolled until the next combat.\r

六百年来，我们一直生活在翁德拉利斯——埃洛克欧拉的地下城。我留下这张便笺，愿未来的世界（我相信人类终将延续）知晓这里发生过什么。我们建立起文明，过着相对安宁的生活。虽然困守于这些洞穴，我们仍辛勤开凿出了属于自己的城市。我们的人生美好而充实。今天，这一切走到了尽头——黑暗领主的孽种正从地上地下同时涌出。大地呕吐出我们前所未见的邪恶生物。在我们上方，曾是盟友的哥布林与巨魔加入了它的军团，将地表翻得支离破碎来搜寻我们。我们无路可逃，而这样的结局我们早已熟悉——许多年前就清楚这会是我们的命运。还剩最后一线希望。扎克伦达找到了某样古老之物……	For 600 years we have lived in Undralis, the undercity of Elok`ora. I leave this note, that the world to come (for I trust that mankind will endure) will know what was done here. We built a civilization, and have lived in relative peace. Although constrained to these caverns, we have toiled and chiseled a city for ourselves. Our lives have been good and full. Today that comes to an end, for the spawn of the dark lord is pouring forth from both above and below. The ground vomits forth vile creatures the likes of which we have never seen before. Above us, the goblins and trolls, once our allies, join his legions and rake the ground apart to find us. There will be no escape for us, yet we have long been acquainted with this ending. It was clear many years ago that this would be our fate. There is one more hope. Zakerunda has found something ancient...

命中：\n进行攻击时，你必须通过一次命中检定。该检定对抗防御者的闪避检定。你的初始命中值由以下公式决定：\r\n\n((敏捷 + 速度) / 2) + (命中 + 武器技能 + 角色等级)\r\n\n进行物理攻击时，初始掷值介于你命中评级的一半与最大命中评级之间。对敌人的了解会加到你的掷值（与伤害）上。公式如下：\r\n\n掷值随机介于 (命中 / 2) 与 ((命中 * 未移动加成) + 知识加成) 之间\r	ToHit:\nWhen making an attack, you must succeed a ToHit roll. This roll is made against the defenders Dodge roll. Your initial ToHit value is determined by the following:\r\n\n((Agility + Speed) / 2) + (ToHit + Weapon Skill + Character Level)\r\n\nWhen making a physical attack the initial roll is a range between half of your ToHit rating and the maximum ToHit rating. Knowledge about an enemy is added to your roll (and damage). The formula is as follows:\r\n\nRandom Roll Between (ToHit / 2) and ((ToHit * No-Move Bonus) + Knowledge Bonus))\r

夹击：\n当同一队伍的两个单位隔着防御者遥遥相对、并进行物理攻击时，他们会获得夹击加成。命中检定与伤害都会提高。\n\n计算方式如下：\n\n命中：+（敏捷 / 2）+ 物品与特质加成\n伤害：+（角色等级 / 2）+ 物品与特质加成\n	Flanking:\nWhen two entities on the same team are directly opposite a defender and making a physical attack, they gain a flanking bonus. ToHit rolls and Damage are both increased.\n\nThe calculations are:\n\nToHit: + (Agility/ 2) + Item and Trait Bonuses\nDamage: + (Character Level / 2) + Item and Trait Bonuses\n

Each one has a specific meaning, and once understood, can be used to identify what kind of magical things is linked to the area you are looking at.\n\nArcane Marks are not meant to be interacted with directly, and do not change based on other conditions.\n\n\n(to avoid Spoilers, this section in a work in progress)

学识加成：\n\n每解锁一项特质，都会在你以该生物为目标时提供额外的伤害加成。这能让你更轻松地清剿非常熟悉的敌人。\n\n类型特质与身体特质尤其有用，因为它们可以在不同物种之间通用。例如，了解「害兽」对老鼠和蜘蛛都有帮助。\n	Lore Bonuses:\n\nEach Trait that you unlock will give you an additional bonus damage when targeting that creature. This can make it easier to clear out enemies that you are very familiar with.\n\nType and Body Traits are especially useful since they can carry over between various Species. For instance, learning about Vermin will help with both rats and spiders.\n

急救技能让你能在战斗结束后立刻为重伤的角色进行少量治疗。\n\n影响属性：速度、抗性	First Aid skill allows you to slightly heal severely injured characters immediately after combat.\n\nModifiers: Speed, Resistance
急救：\n\n指派一名角色进入急救位，可让其运用急救技能，更快地为其他角色疗伤。\n\n这个位置最适合安排在未受伤、或受惠最小的角色身上。\n\n急救者仍会恢复，但速度不如熟睡的人。	First Aid:\n\nAssigning a character to the First Aid slot allows them to use their First Aid skill to heal the other characters faster.\n\nThis slot is best used with a character that is not injured or has less to gain than the others.\n\nThe First Aider will still heal, but not as fast as those who are asleep.

怪物可以拥有 3 类特征：\n\n\n「类型」的例子如害兽、哥布林类或元素生物。每一种类型都有一组与之关联的特征。\n\n「身体」则如石质、虚体或火焰，每一种也有自己的一组特征。\n\n「物种」专属于某一种怪物，不会沿用到其他怪物身上。	There are 3 types of Characteristics that a monster can have:\n\n\nExamples of Type would be Vermin, Goblinoid or Elemental. Each of these Types has a set of Characteristics associated with it.\n\nBody would look like Stone, Ethereal or Fire, and each would have its own set of Characteristics as well.\n\nSpecies are specific to one monster, and do not carry over to others.

整排肋排	Full Rack of Ribs
整整三天，我没有行动，也没有说话。能睡的时候就睡，睡不着时就焦躁不安。\r\n\r\n这样的求生撑不了多久。我必须回去一趟。我拨开灌木，站起身，恐惧攫住了我的心。在我面前站着——不，是飞着——一只我从未见过的生物。它悬停着，像被鹰抓住的蛇。一道光环绕着脸庞旋转，光环上只有一只独眼。它开口说话了。我听不懂它说了什么，因为我的心跳声太响，淹没了一切。\r	For three days I did and said nothing. I slept when I could, and fretted when I could not sleep.\r\n\r\nThis kind of survival could only go on for so long. I had to venture back. I moved the brush, stood, and fear filled my heart. Before me stood... no, flew, a creature unlike any I had ever seen. It hung like a snake caught by an eagle. A halo, with one singular eye, circled its face. And it spoke. I could not understand what it said, for my heart beat so loudly that all else was drowned out.\r

每种特质都有一条进度条。\n\n完成进度后，你会了解到该生物的一些特点——或是一个弱点，或是一项优势。每个物种特质（相对于身体特质或类型特质而言）都会让你更接近解锁该特质。\n\n一旦你解锁了那只怪物的全部物种特质，被锁定的特质就可以用特质点购买了。\n\n	Each Trait has a progress bar.\n\nWhen you complete the progress you learn something about that creature, either a weakness or strength. 点击「信息」按钮可了解更多关于某项法术或能力的信息。	You can find out more about a Spell or Ability by clicking the Info button.
点击「分配」，将你可用的总点数随机但平均地分配出去。	Click on Distribute to do a random but even distribution of your total available points.
点击「技能信息」按钮可了解更多关于某项技能的信息。	You can find out more about a skill by clicking the Skill Info button.
点击「药水」图标即可使用药水及其他物品。	Potions and other items can be used by clicking on the Potion icon.
点击一件物品会显示其数据。右侧的红绿数字表示装备后该项数值会上升还是下降。	Clicking on an item will show you its stats. Red and green on the right indicate whether that items stat will increase or descrease when equipped.
点击一格将其选中。这样做会高亮出该位置在你当前武器射程内的敌人。	Click on a tile to select it. Doing so will highlight enemies that are within range of that position with your current weapon.
点击任意位置，即可移动到那里。	Click anywhere to move to that location.
点击可了解每件物品的详情	Click to find out more about each item
点击可在双手与单手使用该武器之间切换（如可行）。	Clicking this will cycle between using the weapon two-handed and one-handed, if possible.
点击可把与所选物品相同的物品全部汇集起来。	Click this to gather all identical items to the one you have selected.
点击各项属性，可查看增减（+）或（-）它时会影响哪些技能与属性。 \n\n「分配」会将你的点数随机分配。\n\n「随机」不使用点数系统，因此结果可能比分配高得多或低得多。	Select each Stat to see which skills and attributes it will affect as you increase (+)  or decrease (-) it. \n\nDistribute will apply your points randomly.\n\nRandomize does not use the point system and therefore can be much higher or lower than distribution.
点击快捷栏中的图标即可激活。有些需要等待响应，有些则不需要。	Click on an icon in the Quick Bar to activate it. Some require a response, while others do not.
点击敌人发起攻击。	Click on an enemy to make an attack.

疲劳在「偏好设置」中默认为关闭。\n\n在野外露营时，只要你的疲劳条\n尚未见底，你就能恢复生命或法力\n（魔法回复除外）。\n\n休息会在你获得生命与法力的同时\n消除疲劳，但会降低你的恢复速率。\n\n在地图上走动或投入战斗\n都会加重你的疲劳程度。\n战斗越久、敌人越强，\n疲劳条上升得越快。	Tiredness is defaulted to Off in Preferences.\n\nWhen camping in the wild, you can only recover health or mana as long as your Tiredness Meter is above empty (except for magical regeneration).\n\nResting removes tiredness as you gain health and mana, lowering your Recovery Rate.\n\nMoving around a level or engaging in combat will increase your tiredness level. The longer you fight and the harder your enemies, the faster your meter will increase.

目标亡灵会被瞬间消灭，除非其抗魔法与意志力数值通过了对抗该法术等级的技能检定。\n\n通过意志力检定则不受伤害；而以抗魔法正常抵抗该法术，会承受目标最大生命值四分之一的伤害。两种情况下，目标都会陷入恐惧。\n\n抵抗难度随法术等级提高，但你的等级与目标等级差距越大，抵抗也越容易。\n\n结束回合：是	Target Undead is killed instantly unless Resist Magic and Willpower scores beat a skill check against this spell level.\n\nBeating the Willpower check results in no damage, but resisting the spell normally with Resist Magic will deal damage equal to one quarter of the targets Maximum Hit Points. In both cases, the target is Feared.\n\nDifficulty to resist increases with spell levels, but ease to resist increases with the gap between your level and your targets level.\n\nEnds Turn: Yes
目标受到 1.4 点额外伤害，害兽在此基础上再多受 25% 伤害。\n\n抗物理失败的目标被眩晕，持续时间随技能等级延长。\n\n对普通敌人与害兽的额外伤害每级提高 25%。\n\n结束回合：是\n射程：2	Target takes 1.4 bonus damage and Vermin take 25% damage on top of that.\n\nTargets that fail to Resist Physical are Stunned for a duration that increases with skill level.\n\nBonus damage for normal and Vermin are increased 25% per skill level.\n\nEnds Turn: Yes\nRange: 2
目标受到相当于你上次攻击一半的伤害。每轮该伤害再减半，直到降为零或目标抗物理成功。\n\n第一级之后每级使抵抗难度提高 2、起始流血伤害提高 1。\n\n挣脱难度为你法术等级的 5 倍。\n\n结束回合：是	Your target takes damage equal to half of your last attack. Each round that damage is halved again, until it reaches zero or they succeed at Resist Physical.\n\nEach level beyond the first increases the difficulty to resist by 2 and the starting bleed damage by 1.\n\nDifficulty to break is 5 times your Spell level.\n\nEnds Turn: Yes

目标移动至多 3 点移动点的过程中，会在身后留下一串虚界之墙。敌人无法穿越这些墙，它们会持续到目标留下墙壁后的下一回合。\n\n第一级之后每 5 级技能再增加 1 点可留下墙壁的移动点。位移法术的每点移动点也会各自增加一次留墙。\n\n意志力会提高挣脱该法术的难度。\n\n结束回合：否	Target leaves a trail of ethereal walls behind them for up to 3 movement points. These walls cannot be crossed by enemies and last until the targets next turn after leaving them.\n\nEvery five skill levels beyond the first adds an additional movement point to drop walls. 移动：\r\n战斗中，你可以移动等同于移动力的格数，直到你发起攻击或采取其他结束回合的行动为止。\n\n你可以斜向移动，但不能穿过友方或敌方的格子。\r\n\n若你本回合尚未移动，你的命中检定会获得 10% 的「未移动加成」。\n\n寒冷等效果可以把你的移动力降到 1，而晕眩与定身则完全阻止移动。\r	Movement:\r\nDuring combat, you may move a number of spaces equal to your movement, up until the point of making an attack or other turn ending action.\n\nYou may move diagonally, but not through allied or enemy tiles.\r\n\nIf you have not moved this turn, you gain a 10% Non-Movement Bonus to ToHit.\n\nEffects such as cold, can reduce your movement to 1, while stuns and roots prevent movement altogether.\r

符文：\n\n符文是一组刻在石头上的符号。这些符号由一个核心元素（符文最显著的部分）构成，代表符文所系之物——可能是一轮月亮、一个人，甚至别的东西。 \n\n此外还有次级标记，用来表示符文激活所需的额外条件。\n\n（为避免剧透，本节仍在撰写中）	Runes:\n\nRunes are a collection of symbols, etched in stone. These symbols consist of a central element, the most noticable part of the rune, that represents what kind of thing the rune is tied to. This can be a moon, or an individual, or perhaps even something else. \n\nThere are also secondary markings that indicate additional requirements for the rune to activate.\n\n(to avoid Spoilers, this section is a work in progress)

索尔玛利亚到处都是魔法符号。它们主要分为两类：符文与奥术标记。\n\n符文是刻在石头上的符号，会对月亮位置或某些实体的存在等事物产生反应。\n\n奥术标记是强大巫师或魔法事件留下的符号，可以让人一窥此处发生过何种魔法效果。\n	Solmaria is full of magical symbols. There are two main types: Runes and Arcane Marks.\n\nRunes are symbols carved into stone that react to things such as a moon position or the presence of a certain entity.\n\nArcane Marks are symbols left behind by powerful wizards or magical events that give a glimpse into what kind of magical effect takes place.\n

闪避 1	Dodge 1
闪避与防御降低 1，持续 1 轮；伤害提高 1。\n\n提高的伤害在其他计算之后结算。\n\n第一级之后每级再多降低 1 点闪避与防御，并再多提高 1 点伤害。\n\n结束回合：否\n	Reduce Dodge and Defense by 1 and increase Damage by 1 for one round.\n\nIncreased Damage is added after other calculations.\n\nSkill levels beyond the first reduce an additional Dodge and Defense and add an additional Damage.\n\nEnds Turn: No\n
闪避几率提高	Increased Chance to Dodge\t
闪避：\n要避开一次攻击，必须成功通过闪避检定。该检定对抗攻击者的命中检定。\n\n闪避的计算方式为：\n(闪避 + 回避 + 角色等级)\n\n\n\n	Dodge:\nA successful Dodge roll is required to avoid an attack. This roll is made against the attackers ToHit roll.\n\nThe calculation for Dodge is:\n(Dodge + Evasion + Character Level)\n\n\n\n
